- **optimize-global-memory-access**: 2. **Estimate the access efficiency.** For each access: - Ideal: `32 * sizeof(dtype)` bytes useful data per cache line load. - Actual: for stride S, approximately `(32 * sizeof(dtype)) / (S * sizeof(dtype))` = `32/S` useful bytes per cache line, for S &lt;= 32. For S &gt; 32, each thread accesses a different cache line: efficiency = `sizeof(dtype) / 128` (e.g., 4/128 ≈ 3% for fp32).
